const TF_PROTOCOL_T12_JS="/* TaskFlow T12 · Bloqueo de salida exclusivamente mientras un protocolo iniciado esté incompleto.\n * No modifica misiones, sus requisitos, su progreso ni sus datos.\n */\n(function(){\n  'use strict';\n  if(window.__tfProtocolLockT12)return;\n  window.__tfProtocolLockT12=true;\n\n  const PANEL='#windowContent';\n  const normal(value){\n    return String(value||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\\u0300-\\u036f]/g,'')\n      .toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g,' ').trim();\n  }\n  function frame(){return title=normal(h&&h.textContent)||normal(p.querySelector('.v85-routine-modal-copy')?.textContent);\n    const pill=p.querySelector('.v85-routine-progress-pill');\n    const como respaldo cuando el núcleo no presenta contador numérico.\n      const mission=scroll.querySelectorAll('.v94-mentalist-step,.v69-routine-item,.v85-sung-item,.v83-legal-item');\n !!(active&&r&&r.title===active.title&&r.total===active.total);\n  }\n  function update(){\n    if(!active)return;\n    const r=getRoutine();\n    if(!sameRoutine(r))return;\n    const lock=!r.completed;\n    active.completed=r.completed;\n    // Evitar mutaciones DOM innecesarias y no interferir con las tarjetas.\n    if(r.f.classList.contains('tf-protocol-t12-locked')!==lock)\n      r.f.classList.toggle('tf-protocol-t12-locked',lock);\n    if(r.f.classList.contains('tf-protocol-t12-finished')!==!lock)\n      r.f.classList.toggle('tf-protocol-t12-finished',!lock);\n  }\n sigue activo.\n    return !active.completed;\n  }\n  function start(button){\n    const r=getRoutine();\n    if(!r||!r.p.contains(button))return false;\n    if(!START.test(normal(button.textContent)))return false;\n    active={title:r.title,total:r.total,completed:r.completed};\n    update();\n    return true;\n  }\n  function isExit(target,r){\n [aria-label*=\"cerrar\"]'))return true;\n    return !!(r&&!r.p.contains(target));\n  }\n  // Evitar cierres lanzados por la aplicación sin pasar por el botón de la X.\n  // Cuando el 100 % se alcanza, return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error(label)),ms);Promise.resolve(promise).then(value=>{clearTimeout(timer);resolve(value)},error=>{clearTimeout(timer);reject(error)})});\n}\nfunction tf34NativeOpen(){\n return new if(finished){if(db)try{db.close()}catch(_){}return}\n   finished=true;clearTimeout(timer);if(error)reject(error);else resolve(db);\n  }\n  try{\n   if(!window.indexedDB){finish(new Error('IndexedDB no function tf34DirectGet(handle,store,key){\n if(handle.mode==='indexeddb')return tf34Timed(tf34NativeGet(handle.native,store,key),10000,'El almacén de música no respondió.');\n tf34RememberBackend(handle){try{if(handle.mode!=='memory')localStorage.setItem(TF34_BACKEND_KEY,handle.mode)}catch(_){}}\nasync function tf34SelectStore(handle,candidates,error){\n gateProgress(done,total,label){if(!downloadGate)return;downloadBar.style.width=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(done/Math.max(1,total)*100)))+'%';downloadStatus.textContent=label||('Música '+done+' de '+total+' preparada')}\n\nasync function requestPersistentStorage(){try{if(navigator.storage&&navigator.storage.persist)await navigator.storage.persist()}catch(_){}}\nasync function ensureStorageCapacity(){\n  try{\n    if(!navigator.storage||!navigator.storage.estimate)return;\n    const estimate=await navigator.storage.estimate();\n    const required=TRACKS.reduce((sum,t)=>sum+t.expectedSize,0)+8*1024*1024;\n    const available=(Number(estimate.quota)||0)-(Number(estimate.usage)||0);\n    if(available>0&&available<required)throw new Error('No hay suficiente espacio interno para guardar toda la música.');\n  }catch(err){if(err&&String(err.message||'').includes('suficiente espacio'))throw err}\n}\nasync function fetchTrackBlob(track,onProgress){\n let lastError;\n for(let attempt=0;attempt<3;attempt++){\n  const controller=new AbortController();let timer=0,reader=null;\n  const touch=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),20000)};\n  try{\n   touch();\n   const response=await fetch(track.src,{cache:attempt?'reload':'force-cache',credentials:'same-origin',signal:controller.signal});\n   if(!response.ok)throw new Error('No se pudo descargar la música ('+response.status+').');\n   const headerSize=Number(response.headers.get('content-length'))||0;\n   if(headerSize&&headerSize!==track.expectedSize)throw new Error('La música llegó incompleta.');\n   let blob;\n   if(response.body&&response.body.getReader){\n    reader=response.body.getReader();const chunks=[];let received=0;\n    while(true){\n     const part=await reader.read();if(part.done)break;touch();\n     received+=part.value.byteLength;if(received>track.expectedSize)throw new Error('El tamaño de la música no coincide.');\n     chunks.push(part.value);if(onProgress)onProgress(received,track.expectedSize);\n    }\n    blob=new Blob(chunks,{type:response.headers.get('content-type')||'audio/mpeg'});\n   }else{blob=await response.blob();if(onProgress)onProgress(blob.size,track.expectedSize)}\n   if(!(blob instanceof Blob)||blob.size!==track.expectedSize)throw new Error('La descarga de música no terminó completa.');\n   return blob;\n  }catch(error){\n   lastError=error;try{if(reader)await reader.cancel()}catch(_){}\n   if(attempt<2)await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,350*(attempt+1)));\n  }finally{clearTimeout(timer)}\n }\n throw lastError||new Error('No se pudo descargar la música.');\n}\n\nfunction trackSignature(track){return AUDIO_PACK_REV+'|'+track.id+'|'+track.src+'|'+track.expectedSize}\nasync function validateStoredPack(db){\n  const [meta,rows]=await Promise.all([\n    idbGet(db,STORE_META,META_PACK),\n    Promise.all(TRACKS.map(track=>idbGet(db,STORE_TRACKS,track.id)))\n  ]);\n  const missing=TRACKS.filter((track,index)=>{\n    const row=rows[index];\n    return !row||row.signature!==trackSignature(track)||!(row.blob instanceof Blob)||row.blob.size!==track.expectedSize||row.size!==track.expectedSize;\n  });\n  const signatureOk=!!(meta&&meta.signature===PACK_SIGNATURE);\n  return {complete:signatureOk&&missing.length===0,missing,meta};\n}\nasync function cleanupRemovedTracks(db){\n  const valid=new Set(TRACKS.map(t=>t.id));\n  return new Promise((resolve)=>{try{const tx=db.transaction(STORE_TRACKS,'readwrite');const store=tx.objectStore(STORE_TRACKS);const cursor=req.result;if(!cursor)return;if(!valid.has(cursor.key))cursor.delete();cursor.continue()};tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve();tx.onerror=()=>resolve()}catch(_){resolve()}})\n}\nasync function syncAudioPack(force=false){\n if(downloadRunning)return;\n if(audioReady)stopSound();\n downloadRunning=true;audioReady=false;tf34DialogAllowed=false;hideGate();\n let db;\n try{\n  // Comprobar la
